# Inertia head label mappings (`nara.inertia.mappings/1`)

Source: `activity_context/inertia/label_mappings.json` (sha256 `19ae2db05815639e1573c3d83d968fc0c1a41cdc4693e2c98a36f1475c0b16eb`), normalised from Agent C's audited crosswalk (`$INERTIA_DATA_ROOT/inertia-work/agentC/label_mappings.json`, sha256 `480f8831e79a5471121166f695727a3e3d2716295eddcc16a0c6d4bade964a2b`).
Generated by `python -m activity_context.inertia.mappings table`. `MASK` = the head is not supervised by the label (zero loss) — never `unknown`. Sets `{a, b}` = the truth is one of them.
